[EVENT: YEM80485]
Date: 2023-05-21
Country: Yemen | Location: Kara
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Fujayh Tribal Militia (Yemen) [Identity militia]
Actor2: Rashid Munayf Tribal Militia (Yemen) [Identity militia]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Al Mawqea; Crater Sky; Masa Press; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project; YNP [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 21 May 2023, armed groups from al-Fujayh tribe on one side and from al-Rashid Munayf tribe on the other clashed in Kara, Mahattat Bin Maayli (coded to Al Juayshiyah), and Al Qimmad station (Marib, Marib) resulting in fatalities and injuries on both sides as well as the killing of 1 IDP woman. Unspecified number of fatalities coded as 3. Total fatalities coded as 4 and split over 3 locations. 2 fatalities coded to this event including 1 IDP woman. A house and shops were also burned down in Al Musayl. The clash took place two days after previous clashes over the control of Mabshar area were ended by tribal mediation. Mediators brought the new round of clashes to an end and a truce was agreed for eight days after the sides exchanged vehicles and money, along with guarantees from two sheikhs from each side. According to some sources, the al-Hatik tribe fought alongside the al-Rashid Munayf tribe.

---

[EVENT: YEM80539]

[EVENT: YEM80586]
Date: 2023-05-23
Country: Yemen | Location: Bir Mawar
Type: Explosions/Remote violence > Remote explosive/landmine/IED | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: AQAP: Al Qaeda in the Arabian Peninsula [Rebel group]
Actor2: Military Forces of Yemen (2022-2026) Presidential Leadership Council - STC Shabwani Defense Forces [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: An Nusra Media; South24 Center for News and Studies; Twitter; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 May 2023, AQAP militants targeted with an IED a military medical transport vehicle of the STC 1st Shabwani Defense Brigade in Bir Mawar of Wadi Sara (As Said, Shabwah). There were no fatalities or injuries. The vehicle had been dispatched following the injury of the brigade's chief of staff in the area in an AQAP drone attack earlier that day.

[EVENT: YEM80537]
Date: 2023-05-23
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Mukha
Type: Violence against civilians > Abduction/forced disappearance | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: West Coast Joint Forces [Political militia]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Al Mawqea; Yemen Data Project; YNP [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 May 2023, Tareq Saleh's forces (coded as Guardians of the Republic) abducted the staff members and owner of a restaurant in As Samid roundabout in Al Mukha city (Taizz) shutting down the restaurant. Sources claimed that the owner demanded a WCJF commander known as 'Abu Dhiab' to pay a debt the NRF owed, the latter refused and ordered the abduction of the owner and the staff. One source claimed that the debt amounted to 12 million YER. The next day restaurants and stores shut down in solidarity with the arrested civilians.

---

[EVENT: YEM82476]
Date: 2023-05-23
Country: Yemen | Location: Raydan Oil Field
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Aden al Ghad; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 May 2023, members of Al Qazah tribe demonstrated in front of Raydan Oil Field (Marib, Marib) demanding to put a stop to the corruption and the mismanagement of Marib's natural resources that prevent the residents from benefiting from them. The protesters threatened that they will sabotage the gas facilities and the power plants if their demands are not met.

[EVENT: YEM111190]
Date: 2023-05-23
Country: Yemen | Location: As Samsarah
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 May 2023, cargo truck drivers joined a sit-in, organized by the residents of the villages of Dabaa, Al-Safia, and Bani Hammad in As Samsarah (Ash Shamayatayn, Taizz) to denounce the abduction of residents by the Islah-affiliated National Security Bureau in Taizz city the previous summer. The protesters have threatened to close the main road linking Taizz to Al Turbah city in the district if their demands were not met. The sit-in had started on 18 May (coded as a separate event).

---

[EVENT: YEM80468]
Date: 2023-05-23
Country: Yemen | Location: Aden - Maashiq Presidential Palace
Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Yemen) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Abductees Mothers Association; Undisclosed Source [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 23 May 2023, women from Abductees Mothers Association held a protest in front of Aden - Maashiq Presidential Palace (Craiter) to demand the release of 60 forcibly disappeared men. The protestors said that local authorities have ignored their demands for information about their sons for over seven years. The protestors waited for an audience with a government official for several hours with no success.

According to other sources, estimates ranged from 6 to 10 injuries, including the commander of the battalion.

[EVENT: YEM80514]
Date: 2023-05-24
Country: Yemen | Location: Al Masharih

Actor2: Southern Resistance [Political militia]

DESCRIPTION:

[EVENT: YEM93996]
Date: 2023-05-25
Country: Yemen | Location: Sadah
Type: Violence against civilians > Abduction/forced disappearance | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Abductees Mothers Association [Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 May 2023, Houthi forces abducted 2 civilians at Al Amsha checkpoint in Sadah governorate (coded to governorate capital Sadah) (Saadah). They were taken to the Criminal Investigation Department. The reason behind the abduction was not reported. They were then transferred to the Military Intelligence in Saada, where they were subjected to beatings with sticks on all parts of their bodies and then transferred to the Intelligence Prison for two months. They were released in August 2023 following the intervention of mediators and the payment of financial sums by their families.

[EVENT: YEM80471]
Date: 2023-05-25
Country: Yemen | Location: Sanaa
Type: Violence against civilians > Abduction/forced disappearance | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Military Forces of Yemen (2017-) Houthi [State forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: 2 December News; Aden al Ghad; Al Masdar; Al Mashhad Al Yemeni - Pro-IRG; Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Scoop; UN OHCHR; UN Panel of Experts; Undisclosed Source; Yemen Data Project [Local partner-Other]

DESCRIPTION:
On 25 May 2023, Houthi forces abducted 17 Bahais -- including 5 women -- during their yearly meeting in a private residence in Sanaa city (Amanat al Asimah) to elect the community's national governing body. They also raided a number of Bahai houses. A specific reason for the abduction was not reported. However, Houthi authorities have a history of discriminating against and targeting Bahais, as they are considered heretics in Shiite Islam. 11 of the Bahais had been released as of November 2023, while the whereabouts of the 6 others remained unknown. 1 other abductee was released on 22 June 2024. One abductee was released at an unknown date. The last 4 abductees were released on 20 August 2024. Victims explained they had been pressured to renounce their faith through indoctrination via forced participation in 'cultural courses' conducted by Houthi agents.

[EVENT: YEM81060]
Date: 2023-06-29
Country: Yemen | Location: Sadah

Actor2: Civilians (Yemen) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Al Mashhad Al Yemeni - Pro-IRG; Khabar News Agency (Yemen); Mareb Press; Yemen Window [National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 29 June 2023, Houthi forces shot and killed 1 child in Ghalqan area in Sadah city (Saadah, Sadah) after they had kidnapped him and they tortured him for a week including pouring acid on his face. Tribal forces from Monabbih district travelled to Sadah city and attempted to organize a tribal gathering to demand justice in the killing of the boy. One source claimed that the child had previously refused to take part in Houthi activities after a forced recruitment while another source claimed that he had received previous threats from Houthi leaders.
